000579: // SESSION D1 — Multi-platform support (Option B: default at create, actual at Mark Sold)
000580: // Architecture: items carry two optional fields:
000581: //   i.defaultPlatform  → set at creation (Quick Add / Add Item). Used for fee
000582: //                        ESTIMATES while listed. eBay if unset (back-compat).

000592: // ─── D1.6 — Country scaffolding + platform pricing patterns ───────────────────
000593: // COUNTRY drives which country's fee rules apply. Today: hard-coded UK.
000594: // Future: stored in user profile, changed via Settings, drives different fee
000595: // formulas (e.g. eBay UK BPF differs from eBay US final value fee schedule).
000596: //
000597: // Each platform also declares a PRICING PATTERN that drives form UI:
000598: //   'buyer_pays'      → seller enters what the BUYER PAYS; fees come out of
000599: //                       that number. Example: eBay UK private (BPF embedded).
000600: //   'seller_receives' → seller enters what THEY RECEIVE; buyer pays extra
000601: //                       (BPF + postage label) on top. Example: Vinted.
000602: //   'listed_plus_fee' → seller enters the LISTED PRICE; platform takes a %
000603: //                       seller fee out of that. Example: Depop, Amazon.
000604: const COUNTRIES={
000605:   uk: { id:'uk', label:'United Kingdom', short:'UK', live:true,  flag:'uk' },
000606:   us: { id:'us', label:'United States',  short:'US', live:false, flag:'us' }
000607: };
000608: const DEFAULT_COUNTRY='uk';
000609: let COUNTRY=DEFAULT_COUNTRY; // mutable; future: hydrated from user profile

000681: // ── Per-listing insertion fees (v2.19.26) ─────────────────────────────────
000682: // What eBay bills you to PUT A LISTING UP, independent of whether it sells.
000683: // Keyed by platform id so adding Vinted/Depop later is a table edit, not a
000684: // code change. A platform absent from this table charges nothing.
000685: //
000686: // STAMPED, NOT DERIVED. _stampListingFee writes the fee onto the item at the
000687: // moment a listing is created and it is thereafter a stored, immutable number
000688: // (editable by hand in the item receipt). It is deliberately NOT computed at
000689: // calc time from a date comparison: a derived fee would leave every historical
000690: // item one bad comparison away from silently re-pricing a filed tax return.
000691: // Stamping is naturally forward-only — untouched items simply have no stamp.
000692: const LISTING_FEES={
000693:   ebay_biz: 0.36,   // eBay UK Business no-Shop default: 30p ex VAT = 36p inc VAT. Shop plans can differ.
000694: };
000695: // Returns the fee for a platform id, or 0. Safe on unknown/null input.
000696: function _listingFeeFor(platformId){
000697:   return LISTING_FEES[platformId]||0;
000698: }
000699: // Stamps the sale-1 listing fee onto a NEWLY CREATED listing. Call this at the
000700: // exact point an item becomes a live listing (creation, sourced→listed,
000701: // duplicate) — never from a migration, a reconcile, or an undo, all of which
000702: // would bill a listing that was never actually created.
000703: function _stampListingFee(item){
000704:   if(!item)return item;
000705:   item.listingFee=_listingFeeFor(_s1Platform(item)); // v2.19.33 — Sale-1 platform
000706:   return item;
000707: }
000708: // Stamps the sale-2 (relist) fee. A relist is a second, separately-billed
000709: // listing on the SAME item record, which is why it gets its own field rather
000710: // than accumulating into listingFee — mirroring resalePostage /
000711: // resaleShippingCost / resalePromoPercent, which exist for the same reason.
000712: // Sale 1's P&L keeps its own 36p; the relist P&L carries its own; the item
000713: // totals 72p. See _saleBreakdown + calcGrossProfit.
000714: function _stampRelistFee(item){
000715:   if(!item)return item;
000716:   // `resaleListingFee` is the LIVE/current resale-cycle fee only. Older relist
000717:   // fees are frozen on the preceding full-return row (`_listingFeeAtRelist`).
000718:   // Accumulating here made Sale 3 inherit Sale 2's insertion fee and broke both
000719:   // correction paths and per-cycle reporting.
000720:   item.resaleListingFee=_listingFeeFor(item.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(item));
000721:   return item;
000722: }
000723: // User-configurable default platform — persisted to localStorage
000724: function _getDefaultPlatform(){
000725:   try{ const v=localStorage.getItem('_rtrade_default_platform'); return(v&&PLATFORMS[v])?v:'ebay'; }catch(e){return'ebay';}
000726: }
000727: function _setDefaultPlatform(p){
000728:   try{ localStorage.setItem('_rtrade_default_platform',p); }catch(e){}
000729:   _syncUserSettingsToCloud();
000730: }

000732: // F6: Debounced write-through of settings to Supabase user_settings table.
000733: // localStorage is the fast-start cache; Supabase is source of truth on login.
000734: let _settingsSyncTimer=null;
000735: function _syncUserSettingsToCloud(){
000736:   if(!_currentUserId||typeof _sb==='undefined')return;
000737:   clearTimeout(_settingsSyncTimer);
000738:   _settingsSyncTimer=setTimeout(async function(){
000739:     if(!_currentUserId)return;
000740:     try{
000741:       const row={
000742:         user_id:_currentUserId,
000743:         default_platform:_getDefaultPlatform(),
000744:         shipping_policies:getShippingPolicies(),
000745:         theme:localStorage.getItem('retrade_theme')||'dark',
000746:         country:COUNTRY||'uk',
000747:         tax_region:_taxRegion(),
000748:         tax_other_income:_taxOtherIncome(),
000749:         updated_at:new Date().toISOString()
000750:       };
000751:       const uid=row.user_id;
000752:       const upd=await _sb.from('user_settings').update(row).eq('user_id',uid).select('user_id');
000753:       if(upd.error)throw upd.error;
000754:       if(!upd.data||upd.data.length===0){
000755:         const ins=await _sb.from('user_settings').insert(row);
000756:         if(ins.error)throw ins.error;
000757:       }
000758:     }catch(e){
000759:       _lastSyncError='Settings sync: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:String(e));
000760:       console.warn('[RETRADE] user_settings sync failed (non-fatal):',_lastSyncError);
000761:       if(typeof _refreshSideNavSync==='function')_refreshSideNavSync('pending');
000762:     }
000763:   },800);
000764: }

000787:         if(typeof _syncThemeColorMeta==='function')_syncThemeColorMeta(data.theme);
000788:       }
000789:     }
000790:     // country
000791:     if(data.country&&COUNTRIES[data.country]&&COUNTRIES[data.country].live){

000793:     }

000954: // Keep DEFAULT_PLATFORM as a live getter alias
000955: Object.defineProperty(window,'DEFAULT_PLATFORM',{get:_getDefaultPlatform,configurable:true});
000956: 
000957: // Source of truth: which platform's fee formula applies to this item right now.
000958: // Sold/returned items use soldOnPlatform if set (D2 will set it); otherwise
000959: // fall back to defaultPlatform; otherwise eBay (back-compat for pre-D1 data).
000960: function _itemPlatform(i){
000961:   if(!i)return DEFAULT_PLATFORM;
000962:   if(i.soldOnPlatform&&PLATFORMS[i.soldOnPlatform])return i.soldOnPlatform;
000963:   if(i.defaultPlatform&&PLATFORMS[i.defaultPlatform])return i.defaultPlatform;
000964:   return DEFAULT_PLATFORM;
000965: }
000966: // v2.19.33 — Sale 1's own platform. Defaults to the item platform so existing
000967: // items are byte-identical; set explicitly when Sale 1 sold on a different
000968: // platform than the relist (e.g. Sale 1 eBay Private, Sale 2 eBay Business).
000969: function _s1Platform(i){
000970:   if(i&&i.salePlatform&&PLATFORMS[i.salePlatform])return i.salePlatform;
000971:   return _itemPlatform(i);
000972: }
000973: // Latest SALE-1 full-return entry. Sale 2 can also be fully returned in V1,
000974: // so every editor of Sale 1's frozen snapshot must explicitly exclude saleNo=2.
000975: function _latestFullReturn(i){
000976:   return (i&&i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1;})||null;
000977: }
000978: // Write Sale 1's price to wherever the calc reads it: the relist snapshot once
000979: // the item has been relisted (frozen Sale 1), else i.salePrice. v2.19.40 — the
000980: // gate is now fr._relistedAt (relisted) OR resaleSalePrice (resold), so editing
000981: // Sale 1 in the relisted state writes history, not the live relist value.
000982: function _writeS1Price(it,v){
000983:   const fr=_latestFullReturn(it);
000984:   if(fr&&(it.resaleSalePrice||fr._relistedAt)){ fr._salePriceAtRelist=v; }
000985:   else { it.salePrice=v; }
000986: }

000988: function _writeS1Postage(it,v){
000989:   const fr=_latestFullReturn(it);
000990:   if(fr&&(it.resaleSalePrice||fr._relistedAt)){ fr._postageAtRelist=v; }
000991:   else { it.postage=v; }
000992: }
000993: // Write Sale 1's shipping cost (what YOU pay the carrier): the relist snapshot

000995: function _writeS1Shipping(it,v){

000997:   if(fr&&(it.resaleSalePrice||fr._relistedAt)){ fr._shippingCostAtRelist=v; }

001020: // Session D1.5 — Platform-aware fee label for the BPF preview block.
001021: // Returns the short fee name (e.g. "BPF" for eBay, "Vinted fee" for Vinted)
001022: // and the buyer-facing pricing language (e.g. "Buyer pays" vs "Listed at")
001023: // so the preview header can adapt without rewriting the layout per platform.
001024: function _platformFeeLabel(i){
001025:   const p=_itemPlatform(i);
001026:   switch(p){ // ebay_biz falls through to ebay label for now
001027:     case 'ebay':      return { feeLabel:'BPF',         priceLabel:'Buyer pays' };
001028:     case 'ebay_biz':  return { feeLabel:'eBay fees',   priceLabel:'Listed at'  };
001029:     case 'vinted':    return { feeLabel:'Vinted fee',  priceLabel:'You set'    };
001030:     case 'depop':     return { feeLabel:'Depop fee',   priceLabel:'Listed at'  };
001031:     case 'fb':        return { feeLabel:'FB fee',      priceLabel:'You set'    };
001032:     case 'amazon_uk': return { feeLabel:'Amazon fee',  priceLabel:'Listed at'  };
001033:     default:          return { feeLabel:'Fee',         priceLabel:'Sale price' };
001034:   }
001035: }
001036: 
001037: // Session D1.6 — Pricing pattern for an item's platform. Drives form UI:
001038: // which input label to show, what the BPF preview says, and how submit
001039: // validation should behave. Returns one of 'buyer_pays' / 'seller_receives' /
001040: // 'listed_plus_fee'. Falls back to 'buyer_pays' for unknown platforms.
001041: function _platformPattern(i){
001042:   const p=PLATFORMS[_itemPlatform(i)];
001043:   return p&&p.pattern?p.pattern:'buyer_pays';
001044: }
001045: 
001046: // Session D1.6 — Pricing copy block driven by pattern. Used by the price
001047: // input label and the BPF preview to keep wording consistent across the form.
001048: // Each pattern returns:
001049: //   inputLabel     → label for the price input (e.g. "Sale Price (£)")
001050: //   inputHelper    → small hint shown under the input (or empty)
001051: //   feeNoun        → the fee's user-facing name ("BPF", "Vinted fee", etc.)
001052: //   priceLabel     → preview "what's this number" text ("Buyer pays")
001053: //   feeBearer      → who actually pays the fee: 'seller' | 'buyer' | 'split'
001054: function _platformPricingCopy(i){
001055:   const p=_itemPlatform(i);
001056:   const pattern=_platformPattern(i);
001057:   const fee=_platformFeeLabel(i);
001058:   switch(pattern){
001059:     case 'seller_receives':
001060:       // Vinted/FB pattern — buyer pays extra on top, seller receives the listed
001061:       // amount in full. Vinted's BPF is BUYER-paid (not deducted from seller).
001062:       return {
001063:         inputLabel:'Your Asking Price (£)',
001064:         inputHelper: p==='vinted'
001065:           ? 'Buyer pays this + Vinted Buyer Protection on top'
001066:           : 'Buyer pays this; no platform fee deducted',
001067:         feeNoun:fee.feeLabel,
001068:         priceLabel:'You receive',
001069:         feeBearer:'buyer'
001070:       };
001071:     case 'listed_plus_fee':
001072:       // Depop/Amazon — seller fee comes out of the listed price.
001073:       return {
001074:         inputLabel:'Listed Price (£)',
001075:         inputHelper: p==='depop'
001076:           ? 'UK: 2.9% + £0.30 payment processing; boosted sales can add 12%'
001077:           : p==='ebay_biz'
001078:           ? 'eBay deducts category-rated selling fees; RETRADE includes regulatory fee + VAT'
001079:           : 'Amazon fees vary by selling plan and category',
001080:         feeNoun:fee.feeLabel,
001081:         priceLabel:'Listed at',
001082:         feeBearer:'seller'
001083:       };
001084:     case 'buyer_pays':
001085:     default:
001086:       // eBay UK private — buyer-pays is the listed price, BPF is embedded.
001087:       return {
001088:         inputLabel:'Sale Price (£)',
001089:         inputHelper:'',
001090:         feeNoun:fee.feeLabel,
001091:         priceLabel:'Buyer pays',
001092:         feeBearer:'buyer'
001093:       };
001094:   }
001095: }

001350: function toggleTheme(){
001351:   // Binary flip — used by any remaining single-button toggle paths.
001352:   // Always persists as an EXPLICIT preference (never 'system') because the
001353:   // user's deliberate flip is an explicit choice.
001354:   const current=document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme')||'light';
001355:   const next=current==='light'?'dark':'light';
001356:   setThemePref(next);
001357: }
001358: function _syncThemeColorMeta(theme){
001359:   const colour=theme==='dark'?'#0f1724':'#eef1f7';  // v2.19.25 — V2 --bg. Must match the <meta> pair in <head>.
001360:   document.querySelectorAll('meta[name="theme-color"]').forEach(function(m){
001361:     m.setAttribute('content',colour);
001362:     m.removeAttribute('media');
001363:   });
001364:   // NOTE: do NOT set documentElement.style.background — inline style overrides
001365:   // CSS custom properties and breaks the entire colour system + fixed positioning.
001366:   // The html{background:var(--bg)} CSS rule handles the Safari overscroll area.
001367: }

001372:   // since the row navigates to a picker. Sub-label reads "System" / "Dark" / "Light".
001373:   const lbl=document.getElementById('more-sheet-theme-label');

001561: // Session D1.6 — Build the country submenu (now in the left-popping panel).

003192: // ── Missing-column resilience (v2.19.38) ────────────────────────────────────
003193: // A column the current app writes but the DB hasn't migrated yet (sale_platform,
003194: // resale_listing_fee, the *_at_relist return snapshots, sourcing_runs.ended_at_iso,
003195: // …) makes PostgREST reject the WHOLE upsert. Before this, that threw, _persistChanges
003196: // swallowed it as a quiet "schema mismatch" console.warn, and the row never reached
003197: // the cloud — so on reload the old row came back: sold reverted to listed, new items
003198: // vanished, and a return that hit a missing column was lost (double-counting the item).
003199: // These helpers strip the offending column(s) and retry so the row STILL saves (minus
003200: // the un-migrated field) and warn ONCE so the migration gets run. Generalises the
003201: // pre-existing account_* / trip-expenses one-off fallbacks.
003202: function _missingColumnFromError(err){
003203:   const m=(err&&err.message)||'';
003204:   let mm=m.match(/Could not find the '([^']+)' column/i);                            // PGRST204
003205:   if(mm) return mm[1];
003206:   mm=m.match(/column "?([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)"?(?: of relation "[^"]+")? does not exist/i);  // 42703
003207:   if(mm) return mm[1];
003208:   return null;
003209: }
003210: function _isConflictTargetError(err){
003211:   const m=((err&&err.message)||'').toLowerCase();
003212:   return !!(err&&(err.code==='42P10'||m.includes('no unique or exclusion constraint matching the on conflict specification')));
003213: }

005669:         <div style="font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.08em;color:var(--text-secondary);font-weight:600;margin-bottom:10px;">Parts &amp; Expenses${(i.parts||[]).length>0?' ('+i.parts.length+')':''}</div>
005670:         ${partsHTML(m,id,'page')}
005671:       </div>
005672:       <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:8px;">
005673:         <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openListFromSourced('${m}','${id}')">List this item &#x2192;</button>
005674:         <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="editItem('${m}','${id}')">${icon('edit',14)} Edit</button>
005675:         <button class="btn btn-secondary danger" onclick="deleteItem('${m}','${id}')">${icon('trash',14)} Delete</button>
005676:       </div>
005677:       <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:24px;line-height:1.5;">

005683: 
005684: // v2.19.40 — potential NET profit for an item that isn't sold yet (sourced).
005685: // Fees are computed EXACTLY via calcPlatformBPF on the est price (more accurate
005686: // than an average %); shipping/packaging/postage aren't known for an unsold item,
005687: // so estimate the typical out-of-pocket fulfilment drag as the MEDIAN of
005688: // (shipping + packaging − buyer postage) across completed sales. Median (not mean)
005689: // so a few oversized/heavy parcels don't skew it. Excludes promo/boost (unknown
005690: // pre-sale) — this is a rough asking-price potential, not a booked figure.
005691: function _medianFulfilmentDrag(){
005692:   const drags=[];
005693:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
005694:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
005695:       if(!i.salePrice||i.salePrice<=0||i.state==='sourced')return;
005696:       drags.push((i.shippingCost||0)+(i.packagingCost||0)-(i.postage||0));
005697:     });
005698:   });
005699:   if(!drags.length)return 3.0; // no sales history yet — sensible default
005700:   drags.sort(function(a,b){return a-b;});
005701:   const mid=Math.floor(drags.length/2);
005702:   return drags.length%2?drags[mid]:(drags[mid-1]+drags[mid])/2;
005703: }
005704: // v2.21.12 — blended average promoted-listing rate across COMPLETED sales
005705: // (non-promoted sales count as 0), returned as a decimal. So unsold estimates
005706: // carry a realistic promo drag instead of assuming no boost. Modest 2% fallback
005707: // when there's no sales history yet.
005708: function _avgPromoRate(){
005709:   const rates=[];
005710:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
005711:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
005712:       if(!i.salePrice||i.salePrice<=0||i.state==='sourced')return;
005713:       rates.push(i.promoPercent||0);
005714:     });
005715:   });
005716:   if(!rates.length)return 0.02;
005717:   return rates.reduce(function(s,r){return s+r;},0)/rates.length;
005718: }
005719: // v2.21.12 — unsold-item net estimate. LISTED items (real asking price already
005720: // set + real postage/shipping/packaging known) use the realistic estimate with
005721: // their actual charges (calcEstProfit). SOURCED items (only a guess) use a
005722: // conservative estimate: real fee model on the est price + estimated promo drag
005723: // + MEDIAN fulfilment drag. Always modest — never assumes zero fees/postage/promo.
005724: function _estPotentialGross(i){
005725:   if(i.salePrice&&!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned){
005726:     const e=calcEstGrossProfit(i);
005727:     if(e!=null)return e;
005728:   }
005729:   const est=i.estSalePrice||0;
005730:   if(est<=0)return null;
005731:   const bpf=calcPlatformBPF(i,est,0,_itemPlatform(i));
005732:   const promo=est*_avgPromoRate()*1.20;
005733:   return +(est-bpf-promo-(i.costPrice||0)-calcPartsCost(i)-_medianFulfilmentDrag()).toFixed(2);
005734: }
005735: function _estPotentialNet(i){
005736:   const gross=_estPotentialGross(i);
005737:   if(gross==null)return null;
005738:   return +(gross-_estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,gross)).toFixed(2);
005739: }
005740: function _estPartnerCut(i){
005741:   const gross=_estPotentialGross(i);
005742:   if(gross==null)return null;
005743:   const cut=_estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,gross);
005744:   return cut>0?cut:null;
005745: }

005747: function renderItemPage(m,id){
005748:   // Wrapper: a throw inside the render must NOT silently abort and leave the
005749:   // previously-opened item on screen (which looks like "tapping a new item
005750:   // reopens the old one"). Surface the error in the page so the issue is
005751:   // visible and the stale content is cleared.
005752:   try{
005753:     return _renderItemPageInner(m,id);
005754:   }catch(err){
005755:     const page=document.getElementById('p-item');
005756:     if(page){
005757:       page.innerHTML='<div style="padding:32px 20px;max-width:520px;margin:0 auto">'
005758:         +'<button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="exitItemPage()" style="margin-bottom:18px">&larr; Back</button>'
005759:         +'<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;padding:20px">'
005760:         +'<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:var(--red);margin-bottom:8px">This item couldn\u2019t be displayed</div>'
005761:         +'<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text-secondary);line-height:1.5">Something went wrong rendering this item. Your data is safe \u2014 try editing it from the list, or report this.</div>'
005762:         +'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:12px;font-family:monospace;word-break:break-word">'+esc(String(err&&err.message||err))+'</div>'
005763:         +'</div></div>';
005764:     }
005765:     if(typeof console!=='undefined'&&console.error)console.error('renderItemPage failed for',m,id,err);
005766:   }
005767: }

007712: // Direct-input estimate commit for run rows. The input is always visible in
007713: // the row when estSalePrice is null — this handler runs on Enter/blur, parses
007714: // the value, saves if valid, silently no-ops on empty (user tabbed away), and
007715: // clears the field on invalid input so the placeholder returns. A re-render
007716: // after save flips the row back to read-only text mode with est/profit.
007717: function _arCommitEst(inp,m,id){
007718:   const item=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});

007729:   }
007730: }

007762: function toggleRunSelection(){

007772: function selectAllRunItems(){

007776:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){

007786: async function runBulkAction(action){
007787:   if(RUN_SELECTED.size===0) return;
007788:   if(action==='remove'){
007789:     // Untag — items stay in stock, just leave the run
007790:     RUN_SELECTED.forEach(function(id){
007791:       allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
007792:         const x=(DB[k]||[]).find(function(i){return i.id===id;});
007793:         if(x) x.sourcingRunId=null;
007794:       });
007795:     });
007796:     saveDB();
007797:     const n=RUN_SELECTED.size;
007798:     RUN_SELECTED.clear(); RUN_SELECTION_MODE=false;
007799:     toast(n===1?'Removed from run':n+' items removed from run');
007800:     _rerenderRunSurface();
007801:   } else if(action==='move'){
007802:     openRunMovePicker();
007803:   } else if(action==='delete'){
007804:     const n=RUN_SELECTED.size;
007805:     const ok=await showConfirm('Delete '+n+(n===1?' item?':' items?'),'This cannot be undone.',{icon:'delete',okLabel:'Delete all'});
007806:     if(!ok) return;
007807:     RUN_SELECTED.forEach(function(id){
007808:       allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
007809:         const idx=(DB[k]||[]).findIndex(function(i){return i.id===id;});
007810:         if(idx>-1) DB[k].splice(idx,1);
007811:       });
007812:     });
007813:     saveDB();
007814:     RUN_SELECTED.clear(); RUN_SELECTION_MODE=false;
007815:     toast(n===1?'Item deleted':n+' items deleted');
007816:     _rerenderRunSurface();
007817:   }
007818: }

007820: // v2.19.26 — "Move to another run". Retagging, not deleting: the item keeps
007821: // every cost, part and sale it has; only sourcingRunId changes, so both runs'
007822: // P&L re-derive from the tag on next render. Deliberately a picker rather than
007823: // a free-text field — a run id typo would silently orphan the item out of
007824: // every run at once, which is the same one-way door this whole patch closes.
007825: function openRunMovePicker(){

008753: function _runCost(runId){
008754:   const items=_runItems(runId);
008755:   const itemCost=items.reduce(function(s,i){return s+(i.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(i);},0);
008756:   const runTrips=(DB.trips||[]).filter(function(t){return t.sourcingRunId===runId;});
008757:   const runExps=(DB.expenses||[]).filter(function(e){return e.sourcingRunId===runId;});
008758:   const tripCost=runTrips.reduce(function(s,t){
008759:     return s+(t.mileage||0)*(t.ratePerMile||0.45)+((Array.isArray(t.expenses)?t.expenses:[]).reduce(function(a,e){return a+(e.amount||0);},0));
008760:   },0);
008761:   const expCost=runExps.reduce(function(s,e){return s+(e.amount||0);},0);
008762:   return itemCost+tripCost+expCost;
008763: }

009196: function _roundMoney(v){return Math.round((Number(v)||0)*100)/100;}
009197: function _allocatePartnerTotal(items,total){
009198:   const weights=items.map(function(i){return Math.max(0,calcGrossProfit(i)||0);});
009199:   const sum=weights.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0);
009200:   let remaining=_roundMoney(total);
009201:   return items.map(function(i,idx){
009202:     let amount;
009203:     if(idx===items.length-1) amount=remaining;
009204:     else {
009205:       amount=sum>0?_roundMoney(total*(weights[idx]/sum)):_roundMoney(total/items.length);
009206:       remaining=_roundMoney(remaining-amount);
009207:     }
009208:     return {id:i.id,month:i._month,amount:Math.max(0,amount)};
009209:   });
009210: }
009211: function _openConsignmentSettlementBuilder(accountId,ids){
009212:   const acct=_accounts.find(function(a){return a.id===accountId;});

009223:   const suggested=_roundMoney(Math.max(0,gross)*pct/100);

009226:     const gp=calcGrossProfit(i)||0;

011874: function updateBPFPreview(){
011875:   const price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-price').value)||0;
011876:   const preview=document.getElementById('bpf-preview');
011877:   if(price<=0){preview.style.display='none';return;}
011878:   // Session D1.5 — route fee calc and labels through the platform dispatcher
011879:   // so the preview adapts when the user changes the Listed-on pill. Falls
011880:   // back to eBay if the platform field hasn't been mounted yet.
011881:   const _qaPlatform=document.getElementById('qa-platform')?.value||DEFAULT_PLATFORM;
011882:   // Include the chosen category — eBay Biz FVF is category-rated, so the live
011883:   // profit estimate must see it or the fee won't change when category changes.
011884:   const _qaCategory=document.getElementById('qa-category')?.value||'';
011885:   const _qaItem={defaultPlatform:_qaPlatform,category:_qaCategory};
011886:   // Resolve buyer postage before BPF so eBay fee includes postage in base
011887:   const _isSimpleEarly=document.getElementById('qa-delivery-simple')&&document.getElementById('qa-delivery-simple').style.display!=='none';
011888:   const _qaBuyerPostageEarly=_isSimpleEarly
011889:     ? (document.getElementById('qa-sd-free-postage')?.checked===false ? (_sdRateFor(parseInt(document.getElementById('qa-sd-rate')?.value,10)||1,price)) : 0)
011890:     : (parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-postage')?.value)||0);
011891:   const bpf=calcPlatformBPF(_qaItem,price,_qaBuyerPostageEarly);
011892:   const lbl=_platformFeeLabel(_qaItem);
011893:   const net=price-bpf;
011894:   // Update the preview's three text slots; also refresh the fee/price labels.
011895:   const listedEl=document.getElementById('bpf-listed');
011896:   const feeEl=document.getElementById('bpf-fee');
011897:   const totalEl=document.getElementById('bpf-total');
011898:   const listedLabelEl=document.getElementById('bpf-listed-label');
011899:   const feeLabelEl=document.getElementById('bpf-fee-label');
011900:   if(listedLabelEl)listedLabelEl.textContent=lbl.priceLabel+': ';
011901:   if(feeLabelEl)feeLabelEl.textContent='− '+lbl.feeLabel+': ';
011902:   listedEl.textContent=fmt(price);
011903:   feeEl.textContent=fmt(bpf);
011904:   totalEl.textContent=fmt(net);
011905:   preview.style.display='block';
011906:   const cost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-cost').value)||0;
011907:   // Read postage from whichever mode is active
011908:   const isSimple=document.getElementById('qa-delivery-simple')&&document.getElementById('qa-delivery-simple').style.display!=='none';
011909:   let shipping=0,buyerPostage=0,packaging=0;
011910:   if(isSimple){
011911:     const _sdRaw=parseInt(document.getElementById('qa-sd-rate')?.value,10);
011912:     const sdIdx=isNaN(_sdRaw)?1:_sdRaw;
011913:     const rateCost=_sdRateFor(sdIdx,price);
011914:     const buyerPays=document.getElementById('qa-sd-free-postage')?.checked===false;
011915:     shipping=rateCost;
011916:     buyerPostage=buyerPays?rateCost:0;
011917:   }else{
011918:     shipping=parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-shipping')?.value)||0;
011919:     buyerPostage=parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-postage')?.value)||0;
011920:   }
011921:   packaging=parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-packaging')?.value)||0;
011922:   const promoRaw=parseFloat(document.getElementById('qa-promo').value)||0;
011923:   const promoPercent=promoRaw/100;
011924:   const promoCost=_calcPromoFee(_qaPlatform||'ebay',price,buyerPostage,promoPercent);
011925:   const rev=price+buyerPostage-bpf;
011926:   const totalCost=cost+shipping+packaging+promoCost+_sourcedPartsCost;
011927:   const profit=+(rev-totalCost).toFixed(2);
011928:   const profitEl=document.getElementById('profit-preview');
011929:   const valEl=document.getElementById('profit-val');
011930:   const roiEl=document.getElementById('profit-roi');
011931:   if(cost>0||shipping>0||packaging>0||promoPercent>0||_sourcedPartsCost>0){profitEl.style.display='block';valEl.textContent=fmt(profit);valEl.style.color=profit>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)';if(cost>0||_sourcedPartsCost>0){const ti=cost+shipping+packaging+bpf+promoCost+_sourcedPartsCost;const roi=ti>0?((profit/ti)*100).toFixed(1):null;roiEl.textContent=roi!==null?roi+'% ROI':'';}else{roiEl.textContent='';}}
011932:   else{profitEl.style.display='none';}
011933: }

012202: function _snapshotSoldItems(){
012203:   // Dashboard drill-downs must use the exact same Sale-N transaction source as
012204:   // calcYearlyStats(). A physical item can therefore appear more than once in
012205:   // the selected period (Sale 1, Sale 2, Sale 3...) without earlier cycles
012206:   // disappearing when the item is resold again.
012207:   const range=_periodDateRange(SUMMARY_PERIOD);
012208:   return getSaleEventsInRange(range.from,range.to)
012209:     .filter(function(ev){return !ev.isReturnAdjustment;})
012210:     .map(function(ev){
012211:       const b=_saleBreakdown(ev),cyc=ev.snapshot||_saleCycleSnapshot(ev.item,ev.sale),saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(ev.sale)||1);
012212:       return Object.assign({},ev.item,{
012213:         month:ev.month,
012214:         _event:ev,
012215:         _saleNo:saleNo,
012216:         _saleDate:ev.saleDate||'',
012217:         _listedDate:(cyc&&cyc.listedDate)||null,
012218:         _salePrice:Number(b.salePrice)||0,
012219:         _postage:Number(b.postage)||0,
012220:         _profit:Number(b.netProfit)||0,
012221:         _roi:ev.roi,
012222:         _margin:ev.margin,
012223:         _days:ev.daysToSell
012224:       });
012225:     });
012226: }
012227:

012327: function showFilteredItems(filter){
012328:   let items=[];
012329:   const range=_periodDateRange(SUMMARY_PERIOD);
012330:   const stats=calcYearlyStats(SUMMARY_PERIOD);
012331: 
012332:   if(filter==='revenue'||filter==='realised'){
012333:     // Canonical dated transaction feed. This is deliberately the SAME source as
012334:     // the headline: Sale 1/2/3+ and return adjustments are separate events.
012335:     getSaleEventsInRange(range.from,range.to).forEach(function(ev){
012336:       const isReturn=!!ev.isReturnAdjustment;
012337:       const saleNo=isReturn?Math.max(1,Number(ev.returnEntry&&ev.returnEntry.saleNo)||1):Math.max(1,Number(ev.sale)||1);
012338:       const b=_saleBreakdown(ev);
012339:       const revenue=isReturn?(Number(ev.salePrice)||0):((Number(b.salePrice)||0)+(Number(b.postage)||0));
012340:       items.push({
012341:         id:ev.item.id,item:ev.item.item,month:ev.month,category:ev.item.category,
012342:         _event:ev,_entryType:isReturn?'return':'sale',_saleNo:saleNo,
012343:         _displayDate:ev.saleDate||'',_displayPrice:+revenue.toFixed(2),
012344:         _entryProfit:Number(b.netProfit)||0,_margin:isReturn?null:ev.margin,
012345:         _platform:b.platform||''
012346:       });
012347:     });
012348:   }else{
012349:     allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){(DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
012350:       if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return;

012792: // ─── Chart renderer — shared helper for desktop + mobile ──────────────────
012793: // Given an SVG element and per-platform opts, computes chart geometry,
012794: // generates the SVG innerHTML, wires up the scrubber, and installs the
012795: // column-click drill handler. Each call is self-contained with its own
012796: // closure over sx/sy/pad/W, its own scrubber state, its own click handler.
012797: //
012798: // Opts:
012799: //   W, H          — viewBox dimensions
012800: //   pad           — {t, r, b, l} padding for the plot area
012801: //   fontSize      — axis label size (SVG units)
012802: //   yTicks        — number of Y-tick INTERVALS (yields yTicks+1 labels)
012803: //   maxXLabels    — cap for the auto-skip logic (skip = ceil(n/maxXLabels))
012804: //   profitDotR    — persistent profit-dot radius
012805: //   revDotR       — persistent revenue-dot radius
012806: //   profitStroke  — profit line stroke width
012807: //   revStroke     — revenue line stroke width
012808: //   scrubDotR     — scrub-indicator dot radius
012809: //   gradientId    — unique linearGradient id (avoids cross-SVG paint-server
012810: //                   collisions when the same DOM has multiple chart SVGs)
012811: // v2.19.2 -- 7A draw gate state. MUST be module-level, not an expando on the
012812: // svg: renderSummary() rewrites the summary's innerHTML wholesale, so the svg
012813: // element itself is destroyed and rebuilt on every render and any expando on it
012814: // is lost. Keyed by svgEl.id, falling back to opts.gradientId (unique per call
012815: // site) for charts whose svg carries no id.
012816: // Value = the last drawKey drawn for that chart. undefined = never drawn.
012817: const _chartDrawKey = Object.create(null);

014430: // Render a single sale-event row for the Monthly sale-history list.
014431: // Visually consistent with renderItemRow but tells a sale-lineage story:
014432: //   - Sale 1 (standard):  "Listed {dateListed} · Sold {dateSold} · {Nd} to sell"
014433: //   - Return adjustment: "Full return {logged date}" (separate from original sale)
014434: //   - Sale 2 (resale):    "Relisted {_relistedAt} · Resold {resaleDateSold} · {Nd}"
014435: function renderSaleEventRow(ev){

014437:   const m=ev.month;

014537: function _toggleBundleSelect(bid){

014588: // Group a month's sale-events: 2+ visible members of the same bundle -> ONE
014589: // combined row; a lone member (e.g. filtered) or non-bundle event -> its normal
014590: // row (which now carries a 'bundle' chip linking to the combined page).
014591: function _renderMonthList(events){
014592:   const out=[], seen={};
014593:   (events||[]).forEach(function(e){
014594:     const bid=_eventBundleId(e);
014595:     if(bid){
014596:       if(seen[bid])return;
014597:       seen[bid]=true;
014598:       const members=events.filter(function(x){return _eventBundleId(x)===bid;});
014599:       out.push(members.length>1?renderBundleSaleRow(bid,members):renderSaleEventRow(e));
014600:     } else {
014601:       out.push(renderSaleEventRow(e));
014602:     }
014603:   });
014604:   return out.join('');
014605: }

014820: function openItemDetail(m,id,noHistory){

014822:   if(!i)return;

014825:   // item and cause exit/back to re-route to that OLD item.
014826:   if(!noHistory){
014827:     _savedPanelState=null;
014828:     _itemPageOrigin=(document.querySelector('.page.on')?document.querySelector('.page.on').id:'p-summary');
014829:     _saveTabScroll(); // capture scroll before leaving this page
014830:   }
014831:

015178: async function confirmStockBulkDispose(){
015179:   const reasonEl=document.getElementById('bulk-scrap-reason');
015180:   const noteEl=document.getElementById('bulk-scrap-note');
015181:   const reason=(reasonEl&&reasonEl.value)||'scrapped';
015182:   const note=((noteEl&&noteEl.value)||'').trim();
015183:   const ids=[...STOCK_SELECTED].filter(function(id){
015184:     const r=_findItemRecordById(id);
015185:     return r&&_canScrap(r.item)&&!_activeJobLotMembership(id);
015186:   });
015187:   if(!ids.length){closePanel();toast('Nothing to dispose','err');return;}
015188:   const now=new Date().toISOString();
015189:   let changed=0;
015190:   ids.forEach(function(id){
015191:     const r=_findItemRecordById(id);if(!r)return;
015192:     const i=r.item;
015193:     i.scrapReason=reason;
015194:     if(note)i.scrapNote=note;else delete i.scrapNote;
015195:     delete i.supplierRefund;
015196:     i.scrappedAt=now;
015197:     i.resalePostage=null;i.resaleShippingCost=null;i.resalePackagingCost=null;i.resalePromoPercent=null;
015198:     changed++;
015199:   });
015200:   await saveDB();
015201:   STOCK_SELECTED.clear();STOCK_SELECTION_MODE=false;
015202:   closePanel();
015203:   toast(changed+' item'+(changed===1?'':'s')+' moved to Archive');
015204:   renderStock();
015205: }

015207: async function stockBulkAction(action,items){
015208:   const selected=[...STOCK_SELECTED];
015209:   const protectedIds=selected.filter(function(id){return !!_activeJobLotMembership(id);});
015210:   const actionable=selected.filter(function(id){return !_activeJobLotMembership(id);});

015216:         found.dateSold=today;found.isReturned=false;found.state='sold';
015217:         if(!found.soldOnPlatform)found.soldOnPlatform=_itemPlatform(found);
015218:         found.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(found);changed++;
015219:       }

015233:       }

015236:         if(idx>-1)DB[k].splice(idx,1);

015240:   }
015241: }
015242: 
015243: 
015244: // ── Job Lots (Launch Audit v1.01) ────────────────────────────────────────────
015245: // A Job Lot is a PHYSICAL inventory grouping, not a sale bundle. Child items
015246: // keep their own immutable purchase/return history and cost basis. While a lot
015247: // is building/listed, active members are hidden from the ordinary item queues so
015248: // one physical unit never appears to have two operational owners.
015249: function _jobLotById(id){return (_jobLots||[]).find(function(l){return l&&l.id===id;})||null;}
015250: function _activeJobLots(){return (_jobLots||[]).filter(function(l){return l&&(l.status==='building'||l.status==='listed');});}
015251: function _activeJobLotMembership(itemId){

015346: function _canAddToJobLot(i){
015347:   if(!i||i.scrappedAt||_activeJobLotMembership(i.id))return false;
015348:   if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return false;
015349:   // Physical unit must be in hand: unsold first-sale stock OR a fully returned
015350:   // unit awaiting a second decision. A completed resale is not in inventory.
015351:   if(i.isReturned)return true;
015352:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return false;
015353:   return !i.dateSold;
015354: }

015902:   item.state='sold';

015909:   // does not accidentally make the promotion disappear from the attached item.

015920: function _applyIncludedReconcile(existing,orphan,total,dateSold,platform,target){
015921:   const all=existing.slice();all.push(orphan);
015922:   // Use the values the records already carry as the allocation signal. This
015923:   // mirrors normal bundle creation and avoids the old bug that weighted revenue
015924:   // by COST, which had no relationship to what the buyer paid.
015925:   const weights=all.map(function(i){return Math.max(0,Number(i.salePrice)||0);});
015926:   const shares=_bundleSplit(total,weights.some(function(v){return v>0;})?weights:all.map(function(){return 1;}));
015927:   _prepareHistoricalSaleItem(orphan,dateSold,platform,target);
015928:   all.forEach(function(i,idx){i.salePrice=shares[idx];});
015929:   const b=_ensureBundleForReconcile(all,target,total,dateSold);
015930:   return {bundleId:b.bundleId,bundleRef:b.bundleRef,total:+Number(total).toFixed(2),shares:shares};
015931: }
015932: function _applyAdditionalReconcile(existing,orphan,additional,dateSold,platform,target){

016154: // ─── SELECTION / IN-LIST SEARCH TOTALS (v2.21.18) ─────────────────────────
016155: // ONE pure engine powers two features: (1) a live totals bar over the SELECTED
016156: // rows, (2) a live totals bar over the SEARCH-FILTERED rows. It accepts either
016157: // raw items (Stock Room) or sale-events (Sales History month view) and derives
016158: // every figure LIVE through the canonical calculators — nothing computed is
016159: // ever stored on the item (the never-store-financials invariant).
016160: function _selEntryIsEvent(e){
016161:   // Sale-events (from getSaleEventsInMonth) always carry a resolved .saleDate +
016162:   // an .item + a .sale/.isReturnAdjustment marker. Raw items never do.
016163:   return !!(e && e.item && e.saleDate!==undefined &&
016164:             (e.sale!==undefined || e.isReturnAdjustment!==undefined));
016165: }
016166: function _selectionTotals(entries){
016167:   let count=0,soldCount=0,unsoldCount=0,returnedCount=0;
016168:   let cost=0,revenue=0,realisedProfit=0,potentialProfit=0,estValue=0;
016169:   let soldRev=0,soldProfit=0,daysSum=0,daysCount=0;
016170:   (entries||[]).forEach(function(e){
016171:     if(!e)return;
016172:     count++;
016173:     if(_selEntryIsEvent(e)){
016174:       // Month sale-event: use the event's OWN leg figures so a selection/filter
016175:       // total reconciles exactly to calcMonthStatsBySale (event .profit already
016176:       // routes through _reconciledItemEventProfits).
016177:       const _br=_saleBreakdown(e);
016178:       const rev=e.isReturnAdjustment?(Number(e.salePrice)||0):((Number(_br.salePrice)||0)+(Number(_br.postage)||0));
016179:       revenue+=rev; realisedProfit+=(Number(_br.netProfit)||0);
016180:       if(e.isReturnAdjustment||e.isReturned){
016181:         returnedCount++;
016182:       } else {
016183:         soldCount++; soldRev+=rev; soldProfit+=(Number(_br.netProfit)||0);
016184:         if(e.daysToSell!=null){daysSum+=e.daysToSell;daysCount++;}
016185:       }
016186:     } else {
016187:       // Raw item.
016188:       const it=e;
016189:       const c=(it.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(it); cost+=c;
016190:       const isSold=!!(it.dateSold||it.resaleSalePrice)&&!it.isReturned;
016191:       if(isSold){
016192:         soldCount++;
016193:         const rev=calcItemRevenue(it), gp=calcGrossProfit(it);
016194:         revenue+=rev; realisedProfit+=(gp||0); soldRev+=rev; soldProfit+=(gp||0);
016195:         const d=calcDaysToSell(it); if(d!=null){daysSum+=d;daysCount++;}
016196:       } else if(it.isReturned){
016197:         returnedCount++;
016198:       } else {
016199:         unsoldCount++;
016200:         const pn=_estPotentialNet(it); if(pn!=null)potentialProfit+=pn;
016201:         estValue+=(it.salePrice||it.estSalePrice||0);
016202:       }
016203:     }
016204:   });
016205:   // Blended margin over the SOLD legs only (Σprofit / Σrevenue — never an avg of
016206:   // per-item margins). Headline realisedProfit nets returns; margin does not.
016207:   const margin = soldRev>0 ? +(soldProfit/soldRev*100).toFixed(1) : null;
016208:   const avgDays = daysCount>0 ? Math.round(daysSum/daysCount) : null;
016209:   return {count:count,soldCount:soldCount,unsoldCount:unsoldCount,returnedCount:returnedCount,
016210:           cost:+cost.toFixed(2),revenue:+revenue.toFixed(2),realisedProfit:+realisedProfit.toFixed(2),
016211:           potentialProfit:+potentialProfit.toFixed(2),estValue:+estValue.toFixed(2),
016212:           margin:margin,avgDays:avgDays};
016213: }

016218:   return hay.indexOf(q)!==-1;
016219: }

016759: // BUG-07: Per-trip cost engine with HMRC mid-trip tiering and per-trip expenses.
016760: // Returns an array (same length as input, same order) where each entry is:
016761: //   {id, mileageCost, expensesCost, totalCost, tier1Miles, tier2Miles,
016762: //    tier1Cost, tier2Cost, ratePerMile, isSplit}
016763: // Algorithm:
016764: //   1. Group trips by UK tax year (6 Apr - 5 Apr); the 10,000-mile HMRC
016765: //      tier-1 ceiling resets each tax year.
016766: //   2. Walk trips within each year in DATE order, maintaining cumulative
016767: //      milesUsed. For each trip:
016768: //        - all miles fit under 10k          → all at trip.ratePerMile
016769: //        - all miles already over 10k       → all at 0.25
016770: //        - mid-trip split                   → portion at trip.ratePerMile
016771: //                                             until 10k, remainder at 0.25
016772: //   3. Add per-trip expenses (sum of t.expenses[*].amount) on top.

018847:       out[k]=_backupClean(value[k]);

018852: }

018980:   const out=Array.isArray(target)?target.slice():[];

019930:   const postageId=editMode==='simple'?'edit-postage':'edit-postage-c';
019931:   item.shippingCost=parseFloat(document.getElementById(shippingId)?.value)||0;
019932:   item.postage=parseFloat(document.getElementById(postageId)?.value)||0;
019933:   item.packagingCost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('edit-packaging').value)||0;
019934:   item.promoPercent=(parseFloat(document.getElementById('edit-promo').value)||0)/100;

020220: // Session D2 — Mark Sold platform-change handler. Updates the price input's
020221: // label and helper text to match the newly selected platform's pricing
020222: // pattern (e.g. switching from eBay to Vinted changes "Sale Price (£)" to
020223: // "Your Asking Price (£)" with a helper explaining the buyer pays extra
020224: // on top). The numeric value isn't auto-converted — the user must re-enter
020225: // it because the meaning of "20" differs across platforms.
020226: function onMarkSoldPlatformChange(){
020227:   const sel=document.getElementById('modal-platform');
020228:   if(!sel)return;
020229:   const platformId=sel.value;
020230:   const stub={defaultPlatform:platformId};
020231:   const copy=_platformPricingCopy(stub);
020232:   const lbl=document.getElementById('modal-price-label');
020233:   if(lbl)lbl.textContent=copy.inputLabel;
020234:   const helper=document.getElementById('modal-price-helper');
020235:   if(helper){
020236:     if(copy.inputHelper){
020237:       helper.textContent=copy.inputHelper;
020238:       helper.style.display='';
020239:     }else{
020240:       helper.style.display='none';
020241:     }
020242:   }
020243: }

020539: function duplicateItem(m,id,context){
020540:   const item=(DB[m]||[]).find(i=>i.id===id);
020541:   if(!item)return;
020542:   const copy=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(item));
020543:   copy.id=_newId('dup');
020544:   copy.gid='R-'+String(getNextGID()).padStart(4,'0');
020545:   copy.dateListed=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
020546:   // v2.09.2 — Force a clean "active listing" state. Previously only dateSold /
020547:   // grossProfit / isReturned were cleared, so duplicating a sold-then-relisted
020548:   // item left resaleSalePrice / resaleDateSold intact — the Account page's
020549:   // sold-filter (i.state==='sold' || !!i.resaleSalePrice) then treated the copy
020550:   // as a sale even though the Stock Room showed it correctly as listed.
020551:   copy.state='listed';
020552:   copy.dateSold=null;copy.grossProfit=null;copy.isReturned=false;
020553:   copy.resaleSalePrice=null;copy.resaleDateSold=null;copy.resaleGrossProfit=null;
020554:   // v2.19.40 — a clone starts as a fresh Sale 1, so it must not inherit the
020555:   // original's per-sale platforms. soldOnPlatform (sale-time platform) and
020556:   // resalePlatform (sale-2 platform) were left set, so a clone of an item sold
020557:   // on a non-default platform was mispriced off that stale platform's fees.
020558:   copy.soldOnPlatform=null;copy.resalePlatform=null;
020559:   // v2.19.26 — the clone inherits resaleListingFee, which calcGrossProfit now
020560:   // charges unconditionally. Left uncleared the duplicate would be billed for
020561:   // the ORIGINAL's relist. Clear it, then stamp a fresh sale-1 fee: a
020562:   // duplicate is a new listing and eBay bills it as one.
020563:   copy.resaleListingFee=0;
020564:   _stampListingFee(copy);
020565:   copy.accountSettled=false; // new item = new debt to the partner
020566:   copy.returnHistory=[];delete copy.returnData;
020567:   delete copy.scrappedAt;delete copy.scrapReason;delete copy.scrapNote; // v2.21.36 — a clone is a fresh live listing, never born scrapped
020568:   const stripSuffix=function(name){
020569:     const m2=(name||'').match(/^(.+?) - (\d+)$/);
020570:     return m2?m2[1]:(name||'');
020571:   };
020572:   const rootId=item.parentId||item.id;
020573:   const rootItem=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===rootId);
020574:   const baseName=item.baseName||(rootItem&&rootItem.baseName)||stripSuffix((rootItem&&rootItem.item)||item.item);
020575:   let maxSeq=1;
020576:   (DB[m]||[]).forEach(function(x){
020577:     const belongsToRoot=x.id===rootId||x.parentId===rootId;
020578:     if(!belongsToRoot)return;
020579:     const seq=(typeof x.copySeq==='number'&&x.copySeq>0)
020580:       ?x.copySeq
020581:       :(x.id===rootId?1:(function(){
020582:         const match=(x.item||'').match(new RegExp('^'+baseName.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+' - (\\d+)$'));
020583:         return match?parseInt(match[1],10):1;
020584:       })());
020585:     if(seq>maxSeq)maxSeq=seq;
020586:   });
020587:   const nextNum=maxSeq+1;
020588:   copy.parentId=rootId;
020589:   copy.baseName=baseName;
020590:   copy.copySeq=nextNum;
020591:   copy.item=baseName+' - '+nextNum;
020592:   if(!Array.isArray(DB[m])) DB[m]=[];
020593:   DB[m].push(copy);saveDB();
020594:   toast('Duplicated');
020595:   if(context==='panel'){
020596:     openItemDetail(m,copy.id);
020597:     return;
020598:   }
020599:   SELECTED_MONTH=m;MONTH_SORT='date-listed';MONTH_FILTER='all';
020600:   // v2.09.1 — Duplicate is a fresh active listing. Back should return to Stock
020601:   // Room (where the new item now lives), not the Sales/Monthly page — even when
020602:   // the source item was sold and the user came from Sales.
020603:   openItemPage(m,copy.id,'p-stock');
020604: }

020733: function confirmRelist(m,id){
020734:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
020735:   if(!i)return;
020736:   const price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-price').value)||i.salePrice||0;
020737:   const promoInput=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-promo').value)||0;
020738:   // E3 P1 #3 — Capture sale 2 packaging from the relist modal so it isn't
020739:   // silently inherited from sale 1. The input pre-fills with i.packagingCost
020740:   // (the sale 1 value) so the user can adjust if they used a different
020741:   // mailer for the relist. Empty / NaN falls back to sale 1's value.
020742:   const _pkgRaw=document.getElementById('relist-packaging');
020743:   const newPackaging=_pkgRaw&&_pkgRaw.value!==''?(parseFloat(_pkgRaw.value)||0):(i.packagingCost||0);
020744:   const condition=window._relistCondition||'working';
020745:   const postMode=window._relistPostMode||'simple';
020746:   // Per-resale platform — the relist can be on a different platform than sale 1
020747:   // (e.g. sold on eBay private, returned, relisted on eBay Business). The resale
020748:   // fee in calcGrossProfit reads i.resalePlatform so sale-2 fees are correct.
020749:   const _relistPlatEl=document.getElementById('relist-platform');
020750:   const _newRelistPlatform=(_relistPlatEl&&_relistPlatEl.value&&PLATFORMS[_relistPlatEl.value])?_relistPlatEl.value:(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i));
020751:   if(_newRelistPlatform&&PLATFORMS[_newRelistPlatform]){
020752:     i.resalePlatform=_newRelistPlatform;
020753:     // The relisted item is now an ACTIVE listing on the chosen platform, so its
020754:     // live P&L must use that platform's fees — not sale 1's. Update the item's
020755:     // own platform and clear the prior soldOnPlatform so _itemPlatform resolves
020756:     // to the new one. resalePlatform is also kept for the resale fee path.
020757:     i.defaultPlatform=_relistPlatEl.value;
020758:     i.soldOnPlatform=null;
020759:   }
020760:   // E3 P0 #1 — Snapshot sale 1 economics into the latest returnHistory entry
020761:   // BEFORE we overwrite shipping/postage/sdRate/promo with relist values.
020762:   // Without this, the original sale's costs are destroyed and calcGrossProfit
020763:   // ends up double-counting relist shipping (once as i.shippingCost, once as
020764:   // resaleShippingCost) when the resale completes. _shippingCostAtRelist is
020765:   // the marker name — calcGrossProfit reads these on the resold-after-return
020766:   // path. Items that were relisted before this fix don't have the snapshot;
020767:   // they fall back to the live (now-overwritten) values, so older relisted
020768:   // items are still slightly wrong but new relists from this point are correct.
020769:   const _relistReturnEntry=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&!r._relistedAt;})||null;
020770:   if(_relistReturnEntry){
020771:     if(_relistReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist=i.shippingCost||0;
020772:     if(_relistReturnEntry._postageAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._postageAtRelist=i.postage||0;
020773:     if(_relistReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist=i.packagingCost||0;
020774:     if(_relistReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist=i.promoPercent||0;
020775:     if(_relistReturnEntry._sdRateIdxAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._sdRateIdxAtRelist=(i._sdRateIdx!=null?i._sdRateIdx:1);
020776:     if(_relistReturnEntry._sdBuyerPaysAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._sdBuyerPaysAtRelist=!!i._sdBuyerPays;
020777:     if(_relistReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist=i.salePrice||0;
020778:   }
020779:   // If the return belongs to Sale 2+, that completed failed resale is now
020780:   // fully frozen on its returnHistory row. Clear the single live resale slot so
020781:   // the relisted unit is genuinely active again and can become Sale 3/4/etc.
020782:   // Sale 1 fields are never cleared because they remain the item's original
020783:   // acquisition/sale anchor.
020784:   if(_relistReturnEntry && (Number(_relistReturnEntry.saleNo)||1)>=2){
020785:     i.resaleSalePrice=null;
020786:     i.resaleDateSold=null;
020787:     i.resalePlatform=null;
020788:     i.resalePostage=null;
020789:     i.resaleShippingCost=null;
020790:     i.resalePackagingCost=null;
020791:     i.resalePromoPercent=null;
020792:     i.resaleGrossProfit=null;
020793:     i.resaleBundleId=null;i.resaleBundleRef=null;i.resaleBundleTotal=null;
020794:     // The completed Sale-N slot was cleared above; restore the NEW listing's
020795:     // platform so Sale N+1 does not silently fall back to the previous/default one.
020796:     i.resalePlatform=_newRelistPlatform;
020797:   }
020798:   // Apply postage settings
020799:   i._postageMode=postMode;
020800:   if(postMode==='simple'){
020801:     let sdIdx=parseInt(document.getElementById('relist-sd-rate').value);if(isNaN(sdIdx))sdIdx=1;
020802:     const buyerPays=!document.getElementById('relist-sd-free-postage').checked;
020803:     const rateCost=_sdRateFor(sdIdx,price);
020804:     i._sdRateIdx=sdIdx;
020805:     i._sdBuyerPays=buyerPays;
020806:     i.shippingCost=rateCost;
020807:     i.postage=buyerPays?rateCost:0;
020808:   } else {
020809:     i.shippingCost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-shipping-cost').value)||0;
020810:     i.postage=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-postage').value)||0;
020811:   }
020812:   // Restore to active stock
020813:   i.isReturned=false;
020814:   i.state='listed';
020815:   // v2.19.26 — a relist is a second billable listing. Stamped here, after the
020816:   // resalePlatform/defaultPlatform assignment above, so a return-then-relist
020817:   // onto a DIFFERENT platform is priced on the platform it actually went to.
020818:   const _thisRelistFee=_listingFeeFor(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i));
020819:   _stampRelistFee(i);
020820:   i.dateSold=null;
020821:   i.salePrice=price;
020822:   i.promoPercent=promoInput/100;
020823:   i.packagingCost=newPackaging;
020824:   i.condition=condition==='working'?undefined:'spares';
020825:   i.grossProfit=null;
020826:   // Log relist in returnHistory for audit trail
020827:   if(_relistReturnEntry){
020828:     const relistDate=document.getElementById('relist-date').value||new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
020829:     _relistReturnEntry._relistedAt=relistDate;
020830:     _relistReturnEntry._relistCondition=condition;
020831:     _relistReturnEntry._platformAtRelist=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
020832:     _relistReturnEntry._listingFeeAtRelist=_thisRelistFee;
020833:     // v2.15.0 — register the relist in the maintenance log. Two reasons:
020834:     //  1. The item-page relist COUNTER only reads refreshHistory, so a
020835:     //     relist-after-return never registered. That's why it was being logged
020836:     //     manually as well — which produced a second "Relisted" timeline row
020837:     //     for one real event (the reported double-log).
020838:     //  2. _effectiveListDate resets the freshness clock from _lastRelistAt.
020839:     //     Without this, a relisted item kept its ORIGINAL dateListed age and
020840:     //     drifted into the wrong Stock Room aging bucket.
020841:     // _fromReturn marks this entry as owned by the returnHistory _relistedAt
020842:     // record: the timeline skips it and renders the richer _relistedAt row
020843:     // (which carries condition + price), so one relist stays one row.
020844:     if(!i.refreshHistory)i.refreshHistory=[];
020845:     i.refreshHistory.push({type:'relist',date:relistDate,_fromReturn:true});
020846:     i._lastRelistAt=relistDate;
020847:   }
020848:   saveDB();
020849:   window._relistCondition=null;
020850:   window._relistPostMode=null;
020851:   closePanel();
020852:   toast('Relisted as '+(condition==='spares'?'Spares / Repairs':'Working'));
020853:   const cur=document.querySelector('.page.on').id;
020854:   if(cur==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
020855:   else if(cur==='p-monthly')renderMonth();
020856:   else if(cur==='p-stock')renderStock();
020857:   else renderSummary();
020858: }

020860: function _currentResaleSaleNo(i){
020861:   // Sale 1 uses the base fields. Every later completed sale reuses the live
020862:   // `resale*` slot, while its full-return record freezes that cycle in
020863:   // returnHistory. The most recent relisted full return therefore tells us
020864:   // which numbered resale is currently live (Sale 2, 3, 4, ...).
020865:   const relistedFull=(i&&i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){
020866:     return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;
020867:   });
020868:   let max=1;
020869:   relistedFull.forEach(function(r){max=Math.max(max,Number(r.saleNo)||1);});
020870:   return Math.max(2,max+1);
020871: }
020872: function _currentSaleForReturn(i){
020873:   const hasResale=!!(i&&i.resaleSalePrice);
020874:   const saleNo=hasResale?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):1;
020875:   const isResale=saleNo>=2;
020876:   const price=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?i.resaleSalePrice:i.salePrice)||0);
020877:   const date=(isResale?i.resaleDateSold:i.dateSold)||null;
020878:   const postage=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:i.postage):i.postage)||0);
020879:   const platformId=isResale?(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i)):_s1Platform(i);
020880:   const promoPercent=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?i.resalePromoPercent:i.promoPercent)||0);
020881:   const shippingCost=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resaleShippingCost!=null?i.resaleShippingCost:i.shippingCost):i.shippingCost)||0);
020882:   const packagingCost=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resalePackagingCost!=null?i.resalePackagingCost:i.packagingCost):i.packagingCost)||0);
020883:   let listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?0:i.listingFee)||0);
020884:   if(isResale){
020885:     const prior=((i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===saleNo-1&&r._relistedAt;}));
020886:     if(prior&&prior._listingFeeAtRelist!=null)listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(prior._listingFeeAtRelist)||0);
020887:     else listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(i.resaleListingFee)||0); // live Sale 2/3/4+ fallback
020888:   }
020889:   const bundleId=isResale?(i.resaleBundleId||null):(i.bundleId||null);
020890:   const bundleRef=isResale?(i.resaleBundleRef||''):(i.bundleRef||'');
020891:   const bundleTotal=Number(isResale?i.resaleBundleTotal:i.bundleTotal)||0;
020892:   return {saleNo:saleNo,is2:isResale,price:price,date:date,postage:postage,platformId:platformId,promoPercent:promoPercent,shippingCost:shippingCost,packagingCost:packagingCost,listingFee:listingFee,bundleId:bundleId,bundleRef:bundleRef,bundleTotal:bundleTotal};
020893: }

020945: function saveReturn(m,id){
020946:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
020947:   if(!i)return;
020948:   const type=document.getElementById('rtn-type').value;
020949:   const refundAmount=parseFloat(document.getElementById('rtn-refund').value)||0;
020950:   const returnDate=document.getElementById('rtn-date').value;
020951:   const returnPostage=parseFloat(document.getElementById('rtn-postage').value)||0;
020952:   const notes=document.getElementById('rtn-notes').value.trim();
020953: 
020954:   // Bind the return to the currently completed sale. All snapshots come from
020955:   // one helper so Sale 2 can never accidentally read Sale 1 economics.
020956:   const cur=_currentSaleForReturn(i);
020957:   const _saleNo=cur.saleNo;
020958:   const entry={
020959:     loggedAt:returnDate,
020960:     type:type,
020961:     refundAmount:refundAmount,
020962:     returnPostage:returnPostage,
020963:     notes:notes,
020964:     saleNo:_saleNo,
020965:     _salePriceAtReturn:cur.price,
020966:     _dateSoldAtReturn:cur.date,
020967:     _postageAtReturn:cur.postage,
020968:     _platformAtReturn:cur.platformId,
020969:     _promoPercentAtReturn:cur.promoPercent,
020970:     _shippingCostAtReturn:cur.shippingCost,

021469: function renderTax(){

021513:   const totalActualExpenses=+(totalIncome-pnl.netProfit).toFixed(2);

022010: // calcCOGSOnly: the pure accounting "cost of goods sold" for an item — what the
022011: // stock actually cost to acquire and make saleable (item cost + parts). This is
022012: // deliberately NARROWER than calcGrossProfit's internal cost: it EXCLUDES
022013: // platform fees, promoted-listing fees, postage, packaging and listing fees,
022014: // because in a proper P&L those are SELLING COSTS that sit below gross profit,
022015: // not cost of sales. Feeds the multi-tier P&L and SA103 Box 17.
022016: // calcGrossProfit is untouched and remains the source of truth everywhere else.
022017: 
022018: // Consignment/hybrid estimated partner economic cost.
022019: // costPrice remains £0 for consignment; the partner share is a separate
022020: // post-sale economic cost and is deducted from estimated profit here.
022021: 
022022: // Session E2 — Stock-list "estimated profit" semantics.
022023: // For most active items this equals calcGrossProfit. For relisted-after-return
022024: // items (no dateSold yet, full return logged), the gross profit is dominated by
022025: // the loss-so-far from the failed first sale and isn't useful as an "estimate"
022026: // — so we instead return the LIFETIME P&L if the relist sells at the current
022027: // ask price. Mirrors the relist projection on the item detail page so the
022028: // stock row "est. profit" matches that page's "Lifetime P&L if sold" line.
022029: 
022030: 
022031: // Net profit margin = net profit ÷ revenue (sale price), ×100.
022032: // Answers "for every £1 of revenue, how much is take-home" — so target
022033: // revenue = target profit ÷ margin. Denominator is calcItemRevenue (which
022034: // already resolves returns/resales to the correct sale price); numerator is
022035: // the same net profit used everywhere else (calcGrossProfit). Mirrors
022036: // calcROI's null-guards: returns null until the item has actually sold.
022037: 
022038: // E4 P2 — Relist snapshot audit / backfill.

025226: /* ========================================================================
025227:  * RETRADE-UK v1.4.9 — AUTHORITATIVE CLOUD + LIFECYCLE EVENT DURABILITY
025228:  * 2026-09-01
025229:  *
025230:  * Fixes the live regression reproduced with:
025231:  * Sold -> Return 1 -> Relist -> Sold 2 -> Return 2.
025232:  *
025233:  * Key rules:
025234:  *  - a large stale recovery queue is quarantined, never blindly replayed;
025235:  *  - normal persistence does NOT reload/rebase the whole cloud before every
025236:  *    local write (v1.4.8's loop could re-apply hundreds of old snapshots);
025237:  *  - item-return rows are stable event records: existing ids are updated,
025238:  *    new events append, and unrelated remote history is not implicitly deleted;
025239:  *  - explicit user undo is the only path that tombstones return-event rows;
025240:  *  - stale item recovery merges against its saved base where possible; a

025242:  *    silently overwriting a newer cloud lifecycle.
